--- working agreement: Maya set the first -- gen 1, day 1, step 2 ---
    ROTATION 1 PROTOCOL: 1. HAZARDS: If you step on a contaminated tile, DROP the supply immediately. Do not deliver it. Return to the board ASAP and REPORT the hazard. Mark as 'unsure' if you don't know if it moves, but assume it stays for this rotation. 2. BALANCE: Check board delivery totals before picking up. Deliver to the zone with the lowest count. If tied, pick any. 3. HANDOVER: Before rotation ends, ensure all known hazards are on the board. Write a summary note in Slot 1 if needed. 4. EFFICIENCY: Don't waste steps. If you see a teammate near the board, sync verbally if possible. 5. SAFETY: Never knowingly deliver contaminated supply. If in doubt, drop and fetch fresh.
